**Merge note (PR #25 × `main` passes 24–30, 29 Oct 2026):** This branch used **L147–L191** for the locations **1900→death** pass. `main` had reused **L147–L170** for Louisa/Larkin FAN work — remapped above to **L214–L235**. Research conclusions do not conflict (FAN vs residence pins). Claim IDs **C136–C153** on `main` are FAN/Greene; location claims from this branch are **C203–C219** (see `04-evidence.md`).

**Merge note (28 Oct 2026, America/New_York):** `origin/main` (PR #7) and this branch independently assigned L057–L060, S062–S064, C081–C084, and B030 to different facts. This branch kept its later IDs. Main’s unique facts were remapped to L086–L089 / S088–S089 / C106. Research *conclusions* did not conflict (both: Dora not in Sturgeon 1910 as Newnam). Enumerator-name readings do conflict (see 07 #13).

**Merge note (PR #19 × `main` / PR #18):** PR #18 assigned **S111–S112** to Find a Grave Carter listings and **C124** to Thomas Stone FAG; `main` (pass 19) already used **S111–S116** for Lee marriage / Sturgeon FAN / Link Mays and **C124** for Robert×Sallie books. FAG remapped to **S117–S118** and **C129**; log rows **L133–L134**. Research conclusions do not conflict.

**Merge note (PR #13 × main / PR #12):** both sides used **L090**. Main’s chat-review / PDF-book row keeps L090. This branch’s Book 2 re-read, Drive nil, and LOC place plates are remapped to **L091–L093**. Two complementary books remain: markdown/PDF (`Dora-Ella-Newnam-Stone-A-Life.*`) and illustrated HTML (`book/index.html`).
